का . प्रा . 5. -- आयकर अधिनियम , 1961 ( 1961 का 43 ) 
की धारा 122 की उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते 
हुए और इस संबंध में सभी पूर्ववर्ती अधिसूचनाओं का अधिल्लंघन करते 
हुए, केन्द्रीय प्रत्यक्षकर बोर्ड एतद्वारा निदेश देता है कि नीचे दी गई 
अनुसूची के स्तम्भ 1 में विनिर्दिष्ट रेजों के अपीलोब सहायक आयकर 

आयुक्त , आयकर से निर्धारित उन सभी व्यक्तियों और आय को छोड़कर 
जिन पर क्षेत्राधिकार आयकर आयुक्त ( अपील ) में निहित है , उक्त 
अनुसूची के स्तम्भ 2 की तत्संबंधी प्रविष्टि में विनिर्दिष्ट आयकर परि 
मंडलों , बोर्डों और जिलों में , आयकर से निर्धारित सभी व्यक्तियों और 
आय के संबंध में अपने कार्य करेंगे ! 


यतः कोई आयकर परिमण्डल /वार्ड अयत्रा जिला अथवा उसका कोई 
भाग इस अधिसूचना द्वारा एक रेंज से किसी अन्य रेंज में अंतरित कर 
दिया जाता है, उस परिमंडल , वार्ड अथवा जिले अथवा उसके किसी भाग 
में किए गए कर निर्धारणों से उत्पन्न होने वाल. और इस अधिसूचना की 
तारीख से तत्काल पूर्व रेंज के उस अपीलीय सहायक आयुक्त के समक्ष 
विचाराधीन पड़ी अपीलें जिनके अधिकार क्षेत्र से उस परिमंडल, वार्ड 
अथवा जिला अथवा उसका कोई भाग अंतरित किया गया हो , इस अधि 
सूचना के लागू होने की तारीख से रेंज के उस अपीलीय सहायक आयुक्त 
को अंतरित की जायेगी और उसके द्वारा निपटाई जाएंगी , जिसके अधिकार 
क्षेत्र में उक्त परिमंडल , वार्ड अथवा जिला अथवा उसका - कोई भाग 
अंतरित किया गया हो । 
- यह अधिसूचना दिनांक 1 नवम्बर, 1985 से लागू होगी । 
[ सं० 6480 ( फा०सं० 261/ 17/85-प्रा०क० न्या० ]

नधा प्रधिषचना नं . पा . ना . 2,385 तारीन 17 जुलाई 1967 के रूप में 
प्रकाणित जनों का निपनि (निरीक्षण ) नियम , 1967 को उन बातों के

5 . Procedure of inspection.. -- ( 1 ) An exporter intending to 
export a consignmont of footwear or footwear components 
shall submit an intimation for inspection , in writing, to the 
agency of his intention so to do, not less than four days 
prior to the despatch of tho consigoment , to enable the accncy 
to carryout inspection of the consignment as per rule 3 
and the procedure laid down by the Council. 

(2 ) The agency , on satisfying itself that the consigament 
conforms to the standard specifications and requirements of 
thc Act, sholl 199113 an inspection certiflcutes for export within 
four days of reçcipt of the intimation .

8 . Appea ]. - ( 1 ) Any person aggrieved by the refusal of 
the agency to issue a certificate under sub rule ( 3 ) of rule 
5 , may within ten days of the receipt of commun cation of 
such refusal by him prefer an appear to a nanel ot exnerts

( 2 ) The quorum for the panel shall be three . 

( 3 ) The appeal shall be disposed of by the panel within 
fifteen days of its receipt.

बिना बुनी मैटिंग मैट्स 
( क ) सामान्य विशेपताएं :- . फैमिक बुना नहीं जाएगा । यह पस्कनित प्रक्रिया द्वारा नीचे हैसियन या सूती गेज के साथ माधार भूत सम्मिश्रित रखा

2 

_ _ 
पार एम क्यू पी भाई 

पीछे की तरफ रबड़ वाला हसियन मिला समानांतर तथा निकट वस्कनित करके पंश पीर विना 
10 प्रौस शालिटी । 

रूप से 20 स्कोरे , 5 प्लाई धच्चे वाली एंटी प्रशासपीटेड 
में गंधफर बनाए गए । वाली रबड़ लेटेवरा का 

प्रयोग करते हुए प्रस्तरण 
सामग्री से समानांतर गूंथा 

हुआ है - गर्म दबाव ।

( 1 ) " जूस " से सामग्री, या चमरे. कनवम, रबड़, कपड़े , लकड़ी 
श्रावण 

तथा कृतिम भादि जैसी मामग्री के सम्मिश्रण मे पैरों को सुरक्षित रखने के 
लिये तथा कोस्टयूम के मप में मेंडिल, जूते या बूट के प्राकार में बने 

किसी भी प्रकार के जूते प्रा . प्रेत है तथा इसके अन्तर्गत पैदल मैर 
का०मा० 11 - - भारत के निर्यात व्यापार के विकास के लिये के जूते , वर्दी के जूते, सुअवसर के जूते, खेलों के जुने , व्यवमायिक जूते , 
जूते और जूतो के संघटकों को निर्यात से पूर्व क्वालिटी नियंत्रण और विकलांग तथा शल्य जूते मम्मिलित होंगे । 
निरीक्षण के अधीन लाने के लिये कतिपय प्रस्ताव निर्यात ( क्वालिटी 

( 2 ) " जूते संघटकों ” मे सामनो या जूतों के विनिर्माण के प्रयाग 
नियंत्रण और निरीक्षण ) नियम, 1964 के नियम 11 के उप-नियम 

के लिये घमडे , केनवस, रबड़, कपड़े, लकड़ी या कृत्रिम जैसी सामग्री के 
( 2 ) की अपेक्षानुसार, भारत सरकार के वाणिज्य मंत्रालय की अधिसूचना

ORDER 


S . O . 11 .- - Whereas for the development of the export 
trade of India certain proposals for subjecting Footwear and 
Footwear Components to quality control and inspection prior 
to export were published as required by sub -rule ( 2 ) of rule 
11 of the Export ( Quality Control and Inspection ) Rules , 
1964 in the Gazette of India , Part- II, Section 3 , Sub - section 
(ii ) dated the 29th June, 1985 as S . O . 2893 dated the 29th 
June 1985 in suversession of the notification of the Govern 

ment of India in tht. Ministry of Commerce No. S . O . 2384 
dated 17th July , 1967; 


And whereas the objections and suggestions were invited 
from all persons likely to be affccted thereby within forty 
five days of the publication of the said Order in Official 
Gazette ; 

And whereas tile copies of the said Gazette were made 
available to the public on the 31- 7 - 1985 , 

And whereas objections or suggestions received from the 
public oj] the said draft order have been considered by the 
Central Government; 


___ और, ऐसे मभी व्यक्तियों के जिसके उनसे प्रभाषित होने की संभावना 
थी उक्त प्रादेश के राजपन्न में प्रकाशन मे पैतालीम दिन के भीतर प्राक्षेप 
और सुझाव मांगे गये थे , 
__ और उपम राजपत्र की प्रतियां जनता को 31- 7-1985 तक उपलब्ध 
कग दो गई थी ; 

और केन्द्रीय सरकार द्वाग उक्त प्रारूप प्रादेश पर जनता से प्राप्त 
प्राक्षेपों और सुझावों पर विचार कर लिया गया है ; 

असः, अम , केन्द्रीय सरकार, निर्यात ( क्यालिटी नियंक्षण औरनिरीक्षण ) 
अधिनियम , 1963 ( 1963 का 22 ) की धारा 6 द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों 
का प्रयोग करते हुए, तथा उक्त अधिसूचना सं० कामा० 2384 को 
उन बातों के सिवाय अधिकात करते हुए , जिन्हें ऐसे अधिनमण से पहले 
किया गया है या करने का लोप किया गया है, निर्यास निरीक्षण परिषद् 
से परामर्श करने के पश्चात् अपनी यह राय होने पर कि भारत के निर्याप्त 
व्यापार के विकास के लिये ऐसा करना प्रावश्यक और 
समीचीन है , इसके द्वारा :----- 

( 1 ) यह अधिसूषित करती है कि जूते तथा जूतों के संघटको का 
निर्यात से पूर्व क्यालिटी नियंत्रण और निरीक्षण किया जायेगा ; 

( 2 ) जूते तथा जूतों के संघटकों का निर्यास ( क्वालिटी नियंत्रण 
और निरीक्षण ) नियम , 1985 के अनुसार वालिटी नियंत्रण और 
निरीक्षण के प्रकार की क्वालिटी नियंत्रण और निरीक्षण के ऐसे प्रकार के 
म्प में विनिविष्ट करती है जो ऐमे गते तथा जूतों के संघटकों को निर्यात 
मे पूर्ष लागू किया जायेगा ; 

( 3 ) निम्नलिखित को मान्यता देती है : 
( 1 ) गष्ट्रीय तथा अन्तर्गष्ट्रीय मानकों को , या 

( 2 ) विदेशी क्रेता द्वारा अनुमोदित किये गय नमूनों सहित, निर्यात 
कर्ता द्वारा लिखे गए संविधात्मक विनिर्देशों और तकनीकी विनिर्देशों को 
जैसा कि निर्यातकर्ता ने घोषित किया है अने तथा जूतों के संघटकों के 
लिय मामक विनिर्देशों के रूप में । 

( 4 ) अन्तर्राष्ट्रीय व्यापार के अनुक्रम में ऐसे जूते तथा जूतों के 
मंघटकों के निर्यात को सब तक प्रतिषित करती है जब तक कि उसके 
माप उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 7 के अधीम स्थापित अभिकरणों में 
मे मिसी एक द्वारा जारी किया गया इस भाशय का प्रमाण - पन्न न हो 
कि जसे तथा जूतों के संघटकों का परेषण निर्यात योग्य है । 


Now , therefore , in exercise of the power s conferred by 
section 6 of the Export ( Quality Contror and Inspection ) 
Act, 1963 ( 22 of 1963 ) and in supersession of the said noti 
fication No . S .O . 2384 except as respect things done or omitt 
ed to be done before such supersession , the Central Govern 
ment, after consultation with the Export Inspection Council , 
being of Opinion that it is necessary and expedient so to 
do for the developmenl of the export trade of India , hereby. 

( 1 ) Notifics that footwear and footwear components shall 
he subject to quality control and inspection prior to export; 

( 2 ) Specifies the type of quality control and insnection in 
accordance with the Export of Footwear and Footwear Com 
ponents ( Quality Control and Inspection ) Rules, 1985, as 
the type of quality control and inspection which shall te 
applied to such footwear and footwear components prior 10 
export; 


( 3 ) Recognises --- 

(i) national or international standards, or 


(ii ) contractual specifications or technical specifications 

written down by the exporter, alongwith samples ap 
proved by the foreign buyer as declared by the ex 
porter, as the standard specifications for such foot 
wear and footwear components ;

तारीख 15 मार्च, 1984 को , उन बातों के सिवाय अधिक्रांस करते हुए , 
जिन्हें ऐसे प्राधिकरण से पहले किया गया है या करने का लोप किया गया है, 
मीचे की सारणी के स्तं - 1 में उल्लिखित अधिकारी को , जो सरकार का 
राजपत्रित अधिकारी है, उक्त अधिनियम के प्रयोजन के लिए सम्पदा 
अधिकारी नियुक्त करती है, जो अपनी कधिकारिता की स्थानीय सीमाओं 
के भीतर उक्त सारणी के स्तं ( 2 ) की तत्स्थानी प्रविष्टि में विनिर्दिष्ट 
सरकारी स्थानों की बाबत, उक्त अधिनियम द्वारा या उसके अधीन सम्पदा 
अधिकारी फंः प्रदत्त शस्तियों का प्रयोग और अधिरोपित कर्तव्य का पालम 
करेगा ।

ऊर्जा मंत्रालय 


( कोयलाविभाग) 

मई दिल्ली , 17 दिसम्बर , 1985 
का०मा० 30. - केय सरकार ने कोयला घारक क्षेत्र ( अर्जन और विकाम ) अधिनियम , 1957 ( 1957 का 20 ) की घाग 7 को उपधारा ( 1 ) 
के अर्धन भारत के राजपत्र , तार व 16 जुलाई, 1983 में प्रकाशित की गई भारत सरकार के ऊर्जा मंत्रालय ( कोयला विभाग ) की अधिसूचना सं० का०मा० 
2902 तारीख 24 जून , 1983 रा उस अधिसूचना से संलग्न असूर्ष में विनिर्दिष्ट परिक्षत्र में 3288 . 842 एकड़ ( लगभग ) या 1330 . 977 हेक्टर 
( लगभग ) माप पाली भूमि में खनिजों के खनन, खदान, बोर करने, उनकी खुवाई और तलाश करने उन्हें प्राप्त करने , उन पर कार्य करने और उन्हें ये जाने 
के अधिकारों जा अर्जन करने के अपने आशय की सूचना दी थी । 

मोर उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 8 के अन मरण में सक्षम प्राधिकारी ने केन्द्रीय सरकार को अपनी रिपोर्ट दे दी हैं । 

भतः केन्द्र य सरकार का उपरोक्त रिपोर्ट पर विचार करने के पश्चात और मध्य प्रदेश सरकार से परामर्श करने के पश्चाप समाधान हो गया है कि 
इससे संलग्न अनुसूची में वर्णित 1330 . 977 हैक्टर ( गभग ) या 3 283 . 84 ? एकर ( लगभग ) माप वाली भूमि में खनिजों के खनन, खदान , बोर करने 
उनकी खुदाई और उन्हें तलाश करने , उन पर कार्य करने और उन्हें ले जाने के अधिकार का अर्जन किया जाना चाहिए , 

मतः केन्द्रीय मम्भार, उक्त अधिनियम की धाग 9 की उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रक्स शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए, यह घोषण करती है कि इममे सं नग्न 
मार्च में णित 1330 . 977 लेक्टर ( लगभग ) या 3238 , 842 एका ( लगभग ) माप वाली भूमि में अनिजों के खनन, खदानबोर करने और उन्हें नाश 
कारने . प्राप्त करने, उन पर कार्य करने और उन्हें ले जाने के अधिकारों का अर्जन किया गाता है । 

2. इस अधिसूचना के अन्तर्गत आने वाले धन के रेखांक सं० सी01 ( ई )/ HIई आग266-883 तारीख 11- 3-33 का निरक्षण कलक्टर बिनासपुर 
( मध्य प्रदेश ) के कार्यालय में या कोयला नियंत्रक , 1 , काउंमिल हा उम न्ट्रीट , कलमत्ता के कार्यालय में या वेस्ट । कोनफलम लिमिटेड ( रामस्थ अभा ) 
कोल एस्टेट , सिविल लाइन्स, नागपुर - 440001 ( महाराष्ट्र ) के कार्यालय में किया जा सकता है ।

2 अनुपूरक परिवर्धनों की दर कुटुम्ब पेशम से संबंधित होगी क्योंकि 
यह स्कीम के पैरा 12 ( 1 ) के निबंधनों के अनुसार संवेय होगी । 

3. उन पेंशनभोगियों की दशा में , जो तारीख 31- 3- 1984 को स्कीम 
के पैरा 12 ( 2 ) के अधीन पेंशन प्राप्त कर रहे थे, वृद्धि जो पैरा 12 ( 1 ) 
के अधीन उनकी पेंशन से संबंधित है , संगणित की जानी चाहिए और 
उपर्युक्त पैरा 1 के ममूह ( 1 ) और ( 2 ) के लिए 10 ग० प्रतिमास अति 
रिक्त परिवर्धन के साप वृद्धि की उतनी ही मात्रा उस अवधि के दौरान 
संदत्त की जाएगी जिसके लिए पेंशन स्कीम के पैरा 12 ( 2 ) के अधीन 
मंदेय है ।

S. O . 39 . --.In exercise of the powers conferred by paragraph 
37 of the Seamen s Provident Fund Scheme, 1966 , and in super 
Session of the Notification of the Government of India in the 
Ministry of Shipping and Transport ( Transport Wing) No, SW / 
MWS( 28 ) / 82- MT dated 9th March, 1983 . the Central Gov 
ernment, in consultation with the Board of Trustees of the 
Seamen s Provident Fund , hercby fixes with effect from 1st 
August, 1985 the adininistrative charges payable under para 
graph 35 of the said Scheme @ 3 per cent of the total of 
employers and seamen s contribution to the Seamen s Provi 
dent Fund.

The Board of Trustces , Scamen s Provident Fund, in their 
meeting held on 26th July , 1985, have resolved that the ad 
ministrative charges payable under paragraph 35 of the Sea 
men s Provident Fund Schenc, 1966 , shall be paid @ 3 per 
cent of the total of the employers and scamen s contribution 
with effect from 1st August, 1985 . 


In accordance with this resolution the employers are already 
paying the administrative charges @ 3 per cent. It is, there 
Tore, necessary to give retrospoctive cffect to the fixation of 
the rate of administrative churges payable on the Provident 
fund contribution @ 3 per cent with effect from 1st August, 
1985 .

भविष्य निधि आगमन फर्नाटक मेः पुर्व अनुमोदन के बिना नहीं किया जाएगा

होने वाले फायदे किसी रीति से कम हो जाते हैं, तो यह छूट रद्द की 
जा सकती है ।

" Whether the demand of Indian National Coal Mincs 

Engineering Workers Association for employment 
of Shri Nand Kisliore Praşad, S / o . Late Shri 
kishan Dosad , a workman of the Noonidih Jitpur 
Coliery of M / s . Indian Iron and Steal Co. Limit 
ed , who diod following in accident in the Colliery 
during 1971, iş justilied ? If so , to what relief 

is Shri Nand Kishore Prasad ontitled ? " 
The case of the workmen is that late Shri Kishun Dusad 
Was appointed in Noonidih Jitpur Colliery as permanent prop 

uzdoor on 1 - 1 -64. He met with a serious accident whilo 
working underground in 16A seam on 4 -8 -71. He was sent 
to the Central Hospital, Dhanbad for treatment but he died 
in the hospital on 9 - 8 - 71 as a result of the injury he had re 
ceived during the said accident. The said late Kighun Dusad 
left behind him his wife Somari Devi and three sons namoly 
Nand Kishore Prasad , Mahendra Prasad and Manoj Kumar 
at the time of his death . Somari Dovi approached the than 
management to provide lier with somo job. But no employ 
ment was given to her and she was assured that her cldest 
son Nand Kishore Prasad will be provided with a job when 
be attains majority . In 1980 when Shri Nand Kishoro Pra 
sad was aged about 19 years, the union took up the caso of 
his employment in place of his deceased father late Kishun 
Dusad . There was somo defect in the application by which 
an industrial dispute was raiged by the union and as some 
hopeful assurance given by the management, the said indus 
trial dispute was dropped . Thereafter when no fruitful result 
was in sight the present industrial dispute was raised which 
on failwe before the conciliation machinery was referred for 
adjudication by this Tribunal. The dependants of many 
workmen who died as a result of accident whilo in employ 
ment were given employment by the management . Shri 
Suresh Singh , Shri N . Chakravorty and Shri Brihaspati Mad 
dal were given cmployment when their father died in acci 
dent while working. The management of LISCO Ltd . had 
issued a circular dated 26 - 3 - 73 stating that employment will 
be provided to the dependent of the victim of the accident . 
It was not correct to say that the managemen of IISCO , 
were providing employment to the dependents of the cmp 
loyees consequent upon the provision made in NCWA - II in 
1979. The management of JISCO . cyen before nationalisa 
tion had given employment to the dependents of the victims 
of accident on humanitarian ground . On the above plea it 
is submitted that Narid Kishore Prasad , son of fale Kishun 
Dusad , should be given employment by the management.

(PART II - SEC . 3 (11)] 


In view of the discussion made above I hold that the 
demand of Indian National Coal Mines Epgineering Workers 
Association for employment of Shri Nand Kishore Prasad , 
S / o Late Shri Kislian Dosad , a workman of the Noonidih 
Jitpur Colliery of M /s . Indian Iron and Steel Co. Ltd , who 
died following an accident in the Colliery during 1971 is 
not justified . Consequently tho workman is not entitled to 
any relief. 


SCHEDULE 
" Whether the action of the management of Dharmaband 

Colliery of Ms. Bharat Coking Coal Ltd ., in domot 
ing Shri Haroo Pandey from Category - V to category 
IV and allowing Shri S . K . Dey who is junior to 
Shri Haroo Pandey to continue in Category - V is 
justified ? If not, to what relief is Shri Haroo Pandey 
entitled 7" 


This is my Award .

और उनके कर्म कारों के बीच , अनुबंध में निर्दिष्ट औद्योगिक विवाद में 
केन्द्रीय सरकार औद्योगिक प्रधिकरण नं . 2, धनबाद के पंचाट को प्रकाशित 


The case of the workmcn is that the concerned workman 
Shri Haroo Pandey , Electrician of Dharmaband Colliery , 
was upgraded from Category IV to Category V as per recom 
mendation , after his test by duly constituted trade test com 
mitee . The order of his upgradation was communicated to 
him by the General Manager Aroa No. III under his letter 
dated 1920 - 12 -82 , copies of which were endorsed to the 
Agent /Manager , Dharmaband Colliery and the same became 
effective from 1- 11- 82. The Manager Dharmaband Colliery 
withdraw the order of said upgradation by the Office order 
dated 25 - 5 -83 / 1 -6 -83 ag per direction of the General Mana 
ger . It was stated that the upgradation of the concerned work 
man to Category V was due to clerical mistake but the said 
demotion of the concerned workman from Category V to 
Category IV is virtually a demotion of the concerned work 
man . Office order to demote the concerned workman attri 
buting the upgradation to clerical error is misconceived and 
discriminatory , Shri S. K Doy another Electrician of Dharma 
band Colliery who was junior to the conceited workman in 
Category IV was also upgraded to Category V along with 
the concerned workman with effect from 1- 11- 82 . The said 
Shri S . K . Dey has been allowed to continue in Category V 
but the concerned workman has been domoted to Category 
IV on fictitious ground . The said action of the management 
in demoting the concerned workman from Category V to 
Category IV is discriminatory and unjustificd . The present 
reference relates to a disputo on account of demotion of 
the concerned workman from Category V to Category IV 
and it does not relate to a claim from Promotion . The 
promotion of the concerned workman was effected not on 
consideration by the D . P . C . but it was upgradation to Cato 
gory V front Cat. Iy by a duly constituted trade test 
committee . The said trade tost committee examined the con 
cerned workman and recommended for his promotion which 
was accepted by the General Manager of the Area . On the 
abovo plea it is prayed that the action of the management 
is unjustified and that award be passed in favour of the con 
cerned workman reinstating him in the upgraded Category V 
with the difference of wages .

The case of the management is that the promotion is 
the management function and no, workman can claim for 
promotion as a matter of right, he promotion aro guided 
by the rules of promotion framed by the managoment. Any 
promotion dono contrary to the rulcs of promotion is liable 
to be set aside by the court as well as the management. The 
promotion of workman belonging to Electrical and Mechani 
Cal Cadre arc decided according to the rulcs of promotion 
fixed for electrical and mechanical cadre . If a workman gets 
promotion to higher category from a lower category by 
circumventing the rules of promotion , his promotions is 
illegal and is liable to be set aside and the action of the 
management cannot be challenged by the workmen on that 
account. The concerned workman was promoted from the 
Pont of Electrical helper in Category Ili to Electrician in 
Category IV in September 1980 . According to the cadre 
achome ho could be considered for promotion to Category 
V only after completion of 3 years of service as Electrician 
in Category IV i. e . after September , 1983. Thus the concern 
ed workmen was not eligible for promotion in Category V in 
the year 1982 , The D . P . C . constituted in the year 1982 con 
sidered the case of the concerned workman for his promotion 
from Category IV to Category V and was promoted to 
Category V with effect from 1 . 11 - 82 . He had by then comp 
leted only two year ; of experience in Category IV by 
1 -11 - 82 and as such he was not eligible to be considered for 
promotion to Category V . As there was mistake in the pro 
motion of the concerned workman to Category V , the same 
was corrected by cancelling his promotion in Category V . 
Shri S . K . Dey was senior to be concerned workman and 
he was promoted to Category V only after he had complet 
ed 3. years of service in Category IV . It is not a case of 
demotion of the concerned workman from Category V to

or proceduro of selection which can be considered as uniform 
procedure in the matter of selection of Chemists and there 
tore the arguments of the Management that there is no dis 
crimination at all is not correct. Tho Management relied 
upon the decision reported in State of Punjab v, Jagdip Singh 
( AIR 1964 S . C . page 521). It was a case where the Res 
pondent who wory olficiating Tahsildars in the crstwhilo Stato 
of Pepsu vero confimed as Tahsildars with immediate cllect. 
But no posts were however available at that time in which 
the Resondeat could be confirmed . Then the Rajpramukh 
of Pepsu sanctioned the creation of supernumcrary posts of 
Tahsildars to provide lieus for the Tahsildars who had been 
confirmed under the Notification . After the States Reorga 
nisation Act the Punjab Government by virtue of Notifica 
tion de- confirmed the Respondent whereupon the Respondent 
challenged the action . In that context their Lordshins held 
that the order of Financial Commissioner had no legal founda 
tion under the Rules and when there are no vacancies 10 
question of confirmation of the officiating Tahsildars as Tah 
sildars was bad. It is not the case of the Management in the 
instant case that they reverted him back as there was no 
Vacancies. It is their case that they did not comply with tlic 
rules prescribed subsequently showing that he had not ob 
tained 55 % of marks in group with Chemistry as one of 
the subjects and that the practice or procedure adopted by 
lliem in the matter of selection of Chemists was followed in 
his case . The reversion order did not speak anything to 
connect the socalled reversion to any of the reasons attributed , 
On the other hand the worker proved that the rules amended 
subseqeunt to his appointment as a probationer had no appli 
cation to him and that there is no uniform procedure followed 
with reference to internal candidates of the Department when 
they were appointed as Chemist Grade Il. Therefore the 
said ruling had no application to the present facts of the 
case .

11 . The arguments that his appointment was cancelled 
within two months and that it was only a ratification of the 
mistake corsmittel by the Department is not tenable . It is not 
mentioned in the reversion order to that effect. Moreover 
when his application wag recommended by the Head of the 
Hepartment ay conceded by the Management without demand 
ing this criteria of 55 % of marks in group which came later 
they should have considered his application along with other 
Applications if there was such a mistaka Without insisting 
that he is disqualified by virtue of this Circular dated 11 - 3 - 1983. 
Therefore the argument that thcy did not follow the proce 
dure in the case of Mohd . Yakoob Ali s application and that 
there is a set procedure for appointment for such candidates 
is only an after -thought and any amount of arguments deve 
loped on such possibilities when there is no whiaper about the 
same in the reversion order cannot give weight to the Manage 
mients contention, that it is only a retification of mistake . 
First of all there is no mistake and the minutes of selections 
are not placed before this Tribunal and they cannot discrl 
minato him from others who were similarly selected , when 
there are no uniform policy or procedure with reference to 
Internal candidates .

[PART II-- Sec . 3 ( )] 


2. The dispute has arisen becalise of non- payment of uniform 
grade of Rs. 330 - 480 1o all the Turners- cuni-Machinists des 
pite the recommendations of Mehta Committee . It scems that 
a Union culled SPM Mazdoor Şangh , Hoshangabad had served 
strike notice which led to discussions before the Conciliation 
Oficer whose failure of conciliation report culmi 
naicu in the order of reference. At the same time 
despite the issue of notice on receipt of the order of reference 
the Union never cared to appear in the proceedings and the 
contention of the management is that the said union ceased 
to exist which might be the reason for the silçnce observed 
by them . It being so since the order of reference itself being 
conlesícd by the manageincat the points involved will have 
to be cunsidered and determined . 


3 . Originaliy the Security Paper Mill Hoshangabad which is 
a Government of India owned concern producing special 
quality paper for the use of Currency Note Press , had three 
different grades of Turner - von -Machinist viz ., Senior Turner 
cuin -Machinist in the grade of Rs. 380 - 560 ( two posts ) , 
Turner - cum -Machinist Grade- I in the scalc of Rs. 330 -480 
(two posts ) and Turner- cum Machinist Grade II in the grado 
of Rs. 200 -350 ( five posts ) . For the purpose of job evaluation 
of various posts and fixing the strength skill-wise the Govern 
ment of India appoicted an Expert Committee headed by Shri 
R . RI. Mehta who on evaluation made some recomncndations 
but the Government did not agree to implement those recom 
mendations but appointed a technical committce which revised / 
modificd the recommendations in the case of common cats 
gory posts whose recommendations ultimately was approved 
by the Government in the year 1980 . The result of these re 
commendations by Mehta Committee and Technical Committee 
as approved by the Government was the grade of Turner- cum 
Machinist was revised into two grades viz . Turner - cum -Machi 
nists ( H . S . I.) Rs. 380 - 560 ( Two posts ) and Turner- cum -Ma 
chinists ( H . S . II) Rs. 330 - 480 ( seven posts ) . The recommend 
dations were implemented with retrospective effect from 1 - 3 -79 
at that time S /Shri Amar Singh Dhiman and Muralilal Malviya 
were working as H .S .I. grade Rs. 380 - 560 while there were 
Shri Rampath and Shri Bhagchand Omkar Satputc who were 
in H . S .II grade and there were five hands namely Shri J. P . 
Kanskar and four others who were treated as skilled hands 
in the grade of Rs. 260 - 350. It is the case of the management 
that according to the recruitment rules prevalent prior to 
implementation of the Mehta Committee recommendations the 
rost of Senior Turner - cum -Machinist was a selection post to 
be filled 100 per cent by promotion from amongst the Turner 
cum -Machinist Grade I having four years service in the grade 
Rs. 330 - 480 , failing which by selection from other cmployees 
of the Mill possessing the educational and other qualification 
prescribed for direct recruitmeot, failing both by direct recruit 
ment through Employment Exchange . 


years service ho was given the higher grade of Rs. 330 -480 with 
cffect from 17 - 1 - 1983 , Subsequent to the implementation of 
Mehta Committee Technical Committce report two vacancies 
of Turner -cum -Machinist HS- I in the grade of Rs. 330 .480 
had arisen due to promotion of two persons to higher grade , 
but the vacancmy could not be filled in by promotion due to 
non -availability of lower post. Sliri R . Ç . Sagar who was in 
the grade of Rs. 260 -350 was given the sanctioned post of 
Rs. 330 -480 . The qualification for direct recruitment as stated 
was ITI in Turner or Machinist Trade with seven years 
experience in the line in a Machine shop . Since no depart 
mental candidates posessing the requisite qualification were 
available it is stated that persons possessing the technical quali 
fication viz . ITI in Turner Machinist Trade in a lower scale of 
Rs. 260 -350 for four years the intention being that during 
this four year period they would acquire cnough experience 
and expertise in the line so that they can fully share the 
duties and responsibilities of Turner - cum -Machinist Grade - I. 
for cventual promotion to that post, with this view a circula 
was issuel and applications were invited from the depart 
mental candidates but out of five candidațeg who applied twr 
persons were selected namely S Shri R . B . Yadav and Ambik 
P . D . Mansoriu , Mazdoon in thọ unskilled grade of Rs. 196 
232 and they were appointed in the grade of Rs. 260 - 350 
These persons could not have been promotest as Turner -cum 
Machinist Grade in the HS. I] made of Rs. 330 -480 as the 
did not have seven years experience and they were also not 
given the full dutics and responsibilities of H . S .II grade. It 
is also stated that in the normal channel of promotion a 
Mazdoor is promoted to next semi-skilled post in the appro 
priate trade after one year s service and for skilled grade he 
has to work another thrce years in semi- skilled grade and 
hence aprointed in the grace of Rs, 260 - 350 . In the case of 
these workmen they have got a jump in two grades . It is 
thereforo urged that the appointment in the lower grade persons 
of less experience not fulfilling the requirement of Recruitment 
Rules of H .S . N grade, the managemeni s action was justified 
and therefore no relief is permissible . 

6 . On the above pleadings the following issues arise for 
detormination and my findings thereon aro : 
ISSUES 

FINDINGS 
1 . Whether there are two grades of

4 . On behalf of the management an afidavit to this 
effect has been filed by Shri Ramjoewan Agarwal, Partner 
of the Managenient. The affidavit is also supported by tho 
photo copy of the wage register that the said workers are 
still in employment. 

5 . This contention of the management further finds sup 
port by the letter of the Secretary of Metal Mines Workers 
Union dated 3 - 8 - 1985. He says that all the 20 labours pro 
still working with Ms. Central Bihar Construction Company 
And they were neither retrenched nor laid off by tho said 
company. The only change which hag taken place is that 
all these 20 workers have changed their alligiance from 
B .K .M . S . to MM . W .U .

regard . This fact further fortifics the stand of the manago 
ment and the M . M . W . U . Union in the sensc that if tho 
stand of the Bastar Khadan Mazdoor Sangb , Kirandul was 
correct, they would have appcared and led evidenco to provo 
their contentions.

that the management of Ms. Central Bihar Construction 
Company, Bacheli , Owners of the Kirendul Stone Quarry did 
not retrench Shri Etwari Ram and 19 other workmen uptil 
now . There is no question of any relief to the sald workers,
